#### 配置容器

本模块包含 **29** 个配置容器：

##### 1. SoAdBswModules

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `e38bf46e-bcae-4c5a-87d4-3c5269ce8d0d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdBswModules`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific BSW module that the SoAd shall interface to.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **12** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdIf

- **UUID**: `3a92407d-bf4e-4cf0-a911-5335ab35f70f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIf`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the Communication Interface APIs or not. Value true means that the APIs are supported. A module can have both Communication Interface APIs and Transport Protocol APIs (e.g. the PduR module).

######## 2. SoAdIfTriggerTransmit

- **UUID**: `fd1f5968-5e27-4bab-a472-3b9e5453f852`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIfTriggerTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the TriggerTransmit API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 3. SoAdIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `4e2b064d-1665-4f8f-bea0-6f8546424bdd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the TxConfirmation API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 4. SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg

- **UUID**: `e3b2a69d-c684-4fd4-8cc1-b9579289b67c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the LocalIpAddrAssigmentChg API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 5. SoAdSoConModeChg

- **UUID**: `add80472-7f4a-4ced-af43-f25280b8fd29`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSoConModeChg`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the SoConModeChg API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 6. SoAdTp

- **UUID**: `ba22283e-14e3-47a5-9937-b1edd99b28f6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTp`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the TransportProtocol APIs or not. Value true means that the APIs are supported. A module can have both Communication Interface APIs and Transport Protocol APIs (e.g. the PduR module).

######## 7. SoAdUseCallerInfix

- **UUID**: `32151598-c92a-4b21-bee8-0a274e9d657c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdUseCallerInfix`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if SoAd shall use (TRUE) the infix "SoAd" when calling an upper layer module function or not (FALSE). E.g. if SoAdUseCallerInfix is TRUE for the upper layer "ABC" then SoAd will call ABC_SoAdIfRxIndication() otherwise SoAd would call ABC_IfRxIndication().

######## 8. SoAdUseTypeInfix

- **UUID**: `e0f2ae36-c1de-4f2e-b382-753391471792`
- **参数名**: `SoAdUseTypeInfix`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if SoAd shall use (TRUE) the API type infix "Tp" or "If" when calling an upper layer module function or not (FALSE). E.g. if SoAdUseTypeInfix is TRUE for the upper layer "ABC" then SoAd will call ABC_IfRxIndication(), otherwise SoAd would call ABC_RxIndication().

######## 9. SoAdShutdownFinishedCbk

- **UUID**: `9d8b6f7a-471e-40f5-8964-507cc2e73a68`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShutdownFinishedCbk`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter enables a callback which is called if SoAd is in shutdown state after successful call to SoAd_Shutdown().

######## 10. SoAdTpCopyTxDataWithConstPointer

- **UUID**: `f4cb85d7-effb-4b49-87d6-5f0235e20fd7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTpCopyTxDataWithConstPointer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if the upper layer Ul_[SoAd][Tp]CopyTxData() function is implemented with constant pointer for parameter "info".

######## 11. SoAdTpCopyRxDataWithConstPointer

- **UUID**: `46c95bce-d6e9-42cf-86d7-fa0f533aa6ed`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTpCopyRxDataWithConstPointer`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if the upper layer Ul_[SoAd][Tp]CopyRxData() function is implemented with constant pointer for parameter "info".

######## 12. SoAdTpStartOfReceptionWithConstPointer

- **UUID**: `3dc1750e-a20a-4470-9f00-e98156048da7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTpStartOfReceptionWithConstPointer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **描述**: This parameter specifies if the upper layer Ul_[SoAd][Tp]StartOfReception() function is implemented with constant pointer for parameter "info".

##### 2. SoAdConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1184255e-b46f-420e-b4bc-cc7ed0c1c11f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration parameters and sub containers of the AUTOSAR SoAd module. This container is a MultipleConfigurationContainer, i.e. this container and its sub-containers exist once per configuration set.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **16** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdPduRoute

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c32ed2df-5355-4060-bdef-7b09670d11e4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdPduRoute`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Describes the path of a PDU from an upper layer of the SoAd to the socket in the TCP/IP stack for transmission.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **6** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxPduId

- **UUID**: `309a330c-09f0-44f9-bccd-6f89a78b1796`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxPduId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Tx PDU ID of the PDU coming from the PDU Router.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit

- **UUID**: `9a5e32ea-9a84-46a5-a032-e7f7c204b738`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this PDU is transmitted via TriggerTransmit API.
true: On transmission current value is retrieved via call to TriggerTransmit API of upper layer.
false: Transmission is performed in context of transmit service.

In context of nPduUdpTxBuffer this behavior can be used to optimize the nPduUdpTxBuffer and replace it by a request queue.

########## 2. SoAdTxTpOptimized

- **UUID**: `95e35cc7-1656-477c-962f-b3c07c1b5895`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxTpOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for TP transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles transmission in context of SoAd_TpTransmit i.e. TpCopyTxData and TpTxConfirmation of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_TpTransmit.
false: SoAd stores transmission request in SoAd_TpTransmit and handles transmission in SoAd_MainFunction (accroding to AUTOSAR).

########## 3. SoAdTxIfOptimized

- **UUID**: `085f4e65-272c-4d17-8f50-7fbce413cdfd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for IF transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles the transmission in context of SoAd_IfTransmit. This means Ul_[SoAd][If]TxConfirmation and Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_IfTransmit.
false: SoAd stores and forwards transmission requests according to AUTOSAR

########## 4. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall

- **UUID**: `80ce2ab9-3960-442d-8c4a-401300b4e945`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit API is forced to be called only once. In case of a PDU fan-out, SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection, otherwise. If this parameter is enabled, a SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer is used to store the PDU data retrieved by Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit before initiating the PDU fan-out.
true: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit only once.
false: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: ID to be sent on the TCP/IP connection if the PDU header option is enabled.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `ed4eaee5-16cd-4499-9f41-a4c07fae0cf6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the timeout in [s] the nPduUdpTxBuffer shall be transmitted at the latest after this PDU is put into the buffer. This optional parameter is only relevant if SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode is TRIGGER_NEVER.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode

- **UUID**: `348cdb69-8b4e-46cf-b576-8e03b6195e3f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `TRIGGER_NEVER`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `TRIGGER_ALWAYS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGER_NEVER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies whether a PDU triggers the transmission of the nPduUdpTxBuffer. NPduUdpTxBuffer can only be used for upper layers with "IF" API, i.e. this parameter shall only be set if all upper layers belonging to the related socket connection have SoAdTxUpperLayerType set to "IF".
This parameter is only relevant for UDP connections.

TRIGGER_ALWAYS: PDU transmission triggers transmission of nPduUdpTxBuffer
TRIGGER_NEVER: PDU transmission is stored in nPduUdpTxBuffer and triggers nPduUdpTxBuffer transmission only if buffer size is not sufficient

####### 3. SoAdRoutingGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `88f63feb-72a8-400b-818b-2c686daae84e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdRoutingGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific routing group which can be enabled or disabled. A routing group consists of PDUs. Routing of PDUs can either be forwarding of PDUs from the upper layer to a TCP or UDP socket of the TCP/IP stack specified by a SoAdPduRoute or the other way around specified by a SoAdSocketRoute.

####### 4. SoAdSocketConnectionGroup

Specifies the configuration of a socket connection group, i.e. specifies the socket connections belonging to the group and the parameters which are common for all socket connections of the group. A socket connection specifies how data can be received and transmited via a TCP or UDP socket.

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If this feature is enabled the best match algorithm for this socket connection group considers that a socket route is configured for a socket connection.
This means: The best match algortihm will not choose a socket connection which has no receiver even if the remote address matches.

########## 8. SoAdBestMatchWithPduHeaderEnabled

- **UUID**: `3ce92643-63cb-4ebe-9880-b104ead0de5f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBestMatchWithPduHeaderEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If this feature is enabled the best match algorithm of this socket connection group considers the received PDU Header when choosing a socket connection. A received PDU is discarded if no socket route could be found containing the corresponding PDU Header ID.

########## 9. SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline

- **UUID**: `10229585-47b0-45a5-a071-39af3d6be906`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection is kept online on transmission in case automatic socket connection setup is used together with a remote address containing wildcards.
According to AUTOSAR, a socket connection can be opened on UDP reception or on TCP connection establishment. This applies if the configured remote address contains wildcards. After transmission of a PDU, the socket connection shall be closed.

TRUE: The socket connection is not closed on PDU transmission.
FALSE: The socket connection is closed on PDU transmission (standard AUTOSAR behavior).

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The Socket ID is used as a reference to a particular connection when transferring data to and from the PDU Router.

############ 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

############ 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

- **UUID**: `bec3febc-35c5-40a8-97ff-b5eb837dbe9b`

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter enables the usage of the Rx PDU verification callback on this socket connection.
The corresponding callback is configured by parameter SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback.

########## 配置容器

本模块包含 **2** 个配置容器：

########### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

############# BOOLEAN 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote IP address is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: IP address not set,
False: IP address set (valid/wildcard).

############## 2. SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet

- **UUID**: `edde5477-e4e5-4eab-b62c-bc3adac91560`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote port is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: port not set,
False: port set (valid/wildcard).

############# STRING 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress

- **UUID**: `12785fe4-4bc8-4923-bc2d-498de62320d6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

To accept any remote IP address, leave this parameter empty. See message acceptance policy for more details.

- **描述**: Specifies the maximum amout of bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd may queue for transmission via TCP at the TcpIp module for each socket connection of this socket connection group.

(not used)

############ 3. SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `de7fc095-e7c1-4d46-8a6d-c8415ef4f066`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

############ 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

############ 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies to use the keep-alive mechanism for this connection. It will not be 
defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

############ 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies not to use the congestion control mechanism for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval`

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

- **描述**: Specifies the interval in seconds between subsequent keepalive probes.

############ 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in seconds between the last data packet sent and the 
first keepalive probe.

######### 5. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the amout of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

############ 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

############ 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter sets the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

############ 3. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

############ 4. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

############ 5. SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled

- **UUID**: `04b2ba99-08fd-40f5-90ea-19bb116f2ef9`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the reception of broadcasts (IPv4 limited and directed broadcast) and multicasts (IPv6 all nodes and all routers multicast) is enabled. 
The parameter must exist if SoAdSocketApiType is LINUX or QNX and if SoAdSocketLocalAddressRef references a unicast address.
Otherwise, this parameter must not exist.
True: Broadcasts and multicasts shall be received.
False: Broadcasts and multicasts shall not be received.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..320]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

######### 6. SoAdSocketProtocol

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `16b75c1a-d5f9-4eb6-8408-d50cc5051fe0`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketProtocol`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器选择**:

- **SoAdSocketTcp**
  - UUID: `b251c8bd-d43c-4a54-9fed-cc0e7d1e8515`
  - 描述: Specifies that TCP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to TCP socket connections.
- **SoAdSocketUdp**
  - UUID: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
  - 描述: Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

**容器描述**:

Specifies the transport protocol and transport protocol specific parameters used for the socket connections of the socket connection group.

####### 5. SoAdSocketConnection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7d77d0d1-ef5e-4898-81ee-7296f9474dd9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketConnection`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the socket connection (Id and remote address information).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketId

- **UUID**: `846b9a8d-11d9-4e1d-9404-5654dc6f2e74`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The Socket ID is used as a reference to a particular connection when transferring data to and from the PDU Router.

########## 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

########## 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

- **UUID**: `bec3febc-35c5-40a8-97ff-b5eb837dbe9b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter enables the usage of the Rx PDU verification callback on this socket connection.
The corresponding callback is configured by parameter SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **3** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef

- **UUID**: `b55c6185-35e6-45c1-beca-9c52edaa0a0f`
- **引用名**: `SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **目标**: `SoAdBswModules`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/SoAd/SoAdBswModules`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false

- **描述**: Reference to an additional SoAdBswModule which shall be registered to get change notifications (SoConModeChg, IpAddrAssignmentChg) for this socket connection.
Which notification is called depends on specific SoAdBswModule configuration (parameter SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg and SoAdSoConModeChg).
SoAdBswModules which are already registered via SoAdPduRouteDest or SoAdSocketRoute must not be added here.

########## 2. SoAdAdditionalSoConModeChgRef

- **UUID**: `94392887-aaff-49f3-b669-2040e794f9ed`
- **引用名**: `SoAdAdditionalSoConModeChgRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]

- **来源**: Vector Informatik

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a socket connection state change on this socket connection.

########## 3. SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgRef

- **UUID**: `479cc7d8-b6cf-455d-b9f0-892616923b63`
- **引用名**: `SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **目标**: `SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgCallback`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/SoAd/SoAdGeneral/SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgCallback`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a local IP address assignment change on this socket connection.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **2** 个配置容器：

######### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN

######### 2. SoAdTcpTlsConfig

This container contains the TLS configuration for a socket connection. TLS is supported on TCP client sockets only (SoAdSocketTcp/TcpInitiate = TRUE).
If this container does not exist TLS is disabled on this socket connection.

####### 8. SoAdSocketTcp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b251c8bd-d43c-4a54-9fed-cc0e7d1e8515`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketTcp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that TCP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to TCP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **9** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax

- **UUID**: `401eceb8-daf0-4be5-a4b2-20eee480dca5`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Maximum number of times that TCP retransmits an individual data 
segment before aborting the connection.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota

- **UUID**: `4392f2a1-0cf8-4a3e-96d5-ad31f7e7d42f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `4294967295`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum amout of bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd may queue for transmission via TCP at the TcpIp module for each socket connection of this socket connection group.

(not used)

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `de7fc095-e7c1-4d46-8a6d-c8415ef4f066`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies to use the keep-alive mechanism for this connection. It will not be 
defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

########## 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies not to use the congestion control mechanism for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the interval in seconds between subsequent keepalive probes.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7200`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in seconds between the last data packet sent and the 
first keepalive probe.

####### 9. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the amout of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

########## 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter sets the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

########## 3. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

########## 5. SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled

- **UUID**: `04b2ba99-08fd-40f5-90ea-19bb116f2ef9`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the reception of broadcasts (IPv4 limited and directed broadcast) and multicasts (IPv6 all nodes and all routers multicast) is enabled. 
The parameter must exist if SoAdSocketApiType is LINUX or QNX and if SoAdSocketLocalAddressRef references a unicast address.
Otherwise, this parameter must not exist.
True: Broadcasts and multicasts shall be received.
False: Broadcasts and multicasts shall not be received.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..320]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

####### 12. SoAdIpConfig

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides IP related configuration parameter.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c03f63f9-2cb0-49e8-a741-e004ecad4b2d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize

- **UUID**: `e2a5e4b3-4d12-4ae0-9c89-84eaffb32bdc`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `3000`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IP fragmentation buffer.

####### 13. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c03f63f9-2cb0-49e8-a741-e004ecad4b2d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure a trigger transmit buffer.
A trigger transmit buffer is used whenever PDU data has to be stored in SoAd before transmission is possible and SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit is enabled. This applies for example if SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall is enabled for a PDU fan-out or for transmissions via TCP.

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the trigger transmit buffer.

##### 3. SoAdPduRoute

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c32ed2df-5355-4060-bdef-7b09670d11e4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdPduRoute`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Describes the path of a PDU from an upper layer of the SoAd to the socket in the TCP/IP stack for transmission.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **6** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdTxPduId

- **UUID**: `309a330c-09f0-44f9-bccd-6f89a78b1796`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxPduId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Tx PDU ID of the PDU coming from the PDU Router.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit

- **UUID**: `9a5e32ea-9a84-46a5-a032-e7f7c204b738`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this PDU is transmitted via TriggerTransmit API.
true: On transmission current value is retrieved via call to TriggerTransmit API of upper layer.
false: Transmission is performed in context of transmit service.

In context of nPduUdpTxBuffer this behavior can be used to optimize the nPduUdpTxBuffer and replace it by a request queue.

######## 2. SoAdTxTpOptimized

- **UUID**: `95e35cc7-1656-477c-962f-b3c07c1b5895`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxTpOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for TP transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles transmission in context of SoAd_TpTransmit i.e. TpCopyTxData and TpTxConfirmation of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_TpTransmit.
false: SoAd stores transmission request in SoAd_TpTransmit and handles transmission in SoAd_MainFunction (accroding to AUTOSAR).

######## 3. SoAdTxIfOptimized

- **UUID**: `085f4e65-272c-4d17-8f50-7fbce413cdfd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for IF transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles the transmission in context of SoAd_IfTransmit. This means Ul_[SoAd][If]TxConfirmation and Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_IfTransmit.
false: SoAd stores and forwards transmission requests according to AUTOSAR

######## 4. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall

- **UUID**: `80ce2ab9-3960-442d-8c4a-401300b4e945`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit API is forced to be called only once. In case of a PDU fan-out, SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection, otherwise. If this parameter is enabled, a SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer is used to store the PDU data retrieved by Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit before initiating the PDU fan-out.
true: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit only once.
false: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `ed4eaee5-16cd-4499-9f41-a4c07fae0cf6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode

- **UUID**: `348cdb69-8b4e-46cf-b576-8e03b6195e3f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `TRIGGER_NEVER`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `TRIGGER_ALWAYS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGER_NEVER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies whether a PDU triggers the transmission of the nPduUdpTxBuffer. NPduUdpTxBuffer can only be used for upper layers with "IF" API, i.e. this parameter shall only be set if all upper layers belonging to the related socket connection have SoAdTxUpperLayerType set to "IF".
This parameter is only relevant for UDP connections.

TRIGGER_ALWAYS: PDU transmission triggers transmission of nPduUdpTxBuffer
TRIGGER_NEVER: PDU transmission is stored in nPduUdpTxBuffer and triggers nPduUdpTxBuffer transmission only if buffer size is not sufficient

##### 5. SoAdRoutingGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `88f63feb-72a8-400b-818b-2c686daae84e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdRoutingGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific routing group which can be enabled or disabled. A routing group consists of PDUs. Routing of PDUs can either be forwarding of PDUs from the upper layer to a TCP or UDP socket of the TCP/IP stack specified by a SoAdPduRoute or the other way around specified by a SoAdSocketRoute.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Unique ID of Routing Group

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdRoutingGroupIsEnabledAtInit

##### 6. SoAdSocketConnectionGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `88c5e842-9c21-4a26-a037-306f08c46e6b`

**容器描述**:

Specifies the configuration of a socket connection group, i.e. specifies the socket connections belonging to the group and the parameters which are common for all socket connections of the group. A socket connection specifies how data can be received and transmited via a TCP or UDP socket.

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If this feature is enabled the best match algorithm of this socket connection group considers the received PDU Header when choosing a socket connection. A received PDU is discarded if no socket route could be found containing the corresponding PDU Header ID.

######## 9. SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline

- **UUID**: `10229585-47b0-45a5-a071-39af3d6be906`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection is kept online on transmission in case automatic socket connection setup is used together with a remote address containing wildcards.
According to AUTOSAR, a socket connection can be opened on UDP reception or on TCP connection establishment. This applies if the configured remote address contains wildcards. After transmission of a PDU, the socket connection shall be closed.

TRUE: The socket connection is not closed on PDU transmission.
FALSE: The socket connection is closed on PDU transmission (standard AUTOSAR behavior).

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE 类型引用

- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Local IP address and interface used for this connection.

########## 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

########## 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

######### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`

- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote IP address is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: IP address not set,
False: IP address set (valid/wildcard).

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

- **描述**: This parameter defines a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies to use the keep-alive mechanism for this connection. It will not be 
defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

########## 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies not to use the congestion control mechanism for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
