first keepalive probe.

####### 5. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the amout of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

########## 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter sets the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

########## 3. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

########## 5. SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled

- **UUID**: `04b2ba99-08fd-40f5-90ea-19bb116f2ef9`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the reception of broadcasts (IPv4 limited and directed broadcast) and multicasts (IPv6 all nodes and all routers multicast) is enabled. 
The parameter must exist if SoAdSocketApiType is LINUX or QNX and if SoAdSocketLocalAddressRef references a unicast address.
Otherwise, this parameter must not exist.
True: Broadcasts and multicasts shall be received.
False: Broadcasts and multicasts shall not be received.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..320]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

####### 6. SoAdSocketProtocol

- **UUID**: `16b75c1a-d5f9-4eb6-8408-d50cc5051fe0`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

**容器描述**:

Specifies the transport protocol and transport protocol specific parameters used for the socket connections of the socket connection group.

##### 7. SoAdSocketConnection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7d77d0d1-ef5e-4898-81ee-7296f9474dd9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketConnection`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the socket connection (Id and remote address information).

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketId

- **UUID**: `846b9a8d-11d9-4e1d-9404-5654dc6f2e74`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The Socket ID is used as a reference to a particular connection when transferring data to and from the PDU Router.

######## 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

######## 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

- **UUID**: `bec3febc-35c5-40a8-97ff-b5eb837dbe9b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter enables the usage of the Rx PDU verification callback on this socket connection.
The corresponding callback is configured by parameter SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **3** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef

- **UUID**: `b55c6185-35e6-45c1-beca-9c52edaa0a0f`

######## 3. SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgRef

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **2** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote IP address is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: IP address not set,
False: IP address set (valid/wildcard).

########## 2. SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet

- **UUID**: `edde5477-e4e5-4eab-b62c-bc3adac91560`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false

##### 10. SoAdSocketTcp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b251c8bd-d43c-4a54-9fed-cc0e7d1e8515`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketTcp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that TCP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to TCP socket connections.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **9** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax

- **UUID**: `401eceb8-daf0-4be5-a4b2-20eee480dca5`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Maximum number of times that TCP retransmits an individual data 
segment before aborting the connection.

######## 2. SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota

- **UUID**: `4392f2a1-0cf8-4a3e-96d5-ad31f7e7d42f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `4294967295`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum amout of bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd may queue for transmission via TCP at the TcpIp module for each socket connection of this socket connection group.

(not used)

######## 3. SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `de7fc095-e7c1-4d46-8a6d-c8415ef4f066`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

######## 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

######## 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies to use the keep-alive mechanism for this connection. It will not be 
defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

######## 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies not to use the congestion control mechanism for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the interval in seconds between subsequent keepalive probes.

######## 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7200`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in seconds between the last data packet sent and the 
first keepalive probe.

##### 11. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the amout of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

######## 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

######## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter sets the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

######## 3. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

######## 4. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

######## 5. SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled

- **UUID**: `04b2ba99-08fd-40f5-90ea-19bb116f2ef9`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpBroadcastReceptionEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the reception of broadcasts (IPv4 limited and directed broadcast) and multicasts (IPv6 all nodes and all routers multicast) is enabled. 
The parameter must exist if SoAdSocketApiType is LINUX or QNX and if SoAdSocketLocalAddressRef references a unicast address.
Otherwise, this parameter must not exist.
True: Broadcasts and multicasts shall be received.
False: Broadcasts and multicasts shall not be received.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..320]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

##### 14. SoAdIpConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `191e85a2-3ab4-4b55-8843-9ffe15cd5020`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides IP related configuration parameter.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c03f63f9-2cb0-49e8-a741-e004ecad4b2d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize

- **UUID**: `e2a5e4b3-4d12-4ae0-9c89-84eaffb32bdc`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `3000`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IP fragmentation buffer.

##### 15. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c03f63f9-2cb0-49e8-a741-e004ecad4b2d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER

- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **构建后可变**: true

This container provides parameter to configure a trigger transmit buffer.
A trigger transmit buffer is used whenever PDU data has to be stored in SoAd before transmission is possible and SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit is enabled. This applies for example if SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall is enabled for a PDU fan-out or for transmissions via TCP.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if TLS communication shall be enabled for SoAd.

######## 5. SoAdTxDynamicLengthEnabled

- **UUID**: `f4559e8f-40c0-4473-abeb-671a60b9fae6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxDynamicLengthEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if data length can be modified or set during transmission process.
In case of transmission via IfRoutingGroupTrannsmit API no PDU length is known until call to TriggerTransmit callback to retrieve data from upper layer. Therefore an internal buffer is used to store the PDU first before transmit function of TcpIp module can be called. If this feature is enabled the mentioned buffer is not needed anymore since TcpIp module can handle setting length on transmission.
In case of transmission via IfTriggerTransmit API length can differ between transmission request and TriggerTransmit callback. If this feature is not enabled such transmissions are aborted.
In case of nPdu and if IfTriggerTransmit is enabled on the corresponding SoAdPduRoutes nPduUdpTxBuffer can be optimized and is replaced by a request queue if this feature is enabled.

######## 6. SoAdMainFunctionSplitEnabled

- **UUID**: `e1e27e02-ff9a-42c0-ace0-438eca534828`
- **参数名**: `SoAdMainFunctionSplitEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies whether SoAd shall split up the SoAd_MainFunction into three functions:

SoAd_MainFunctionRx
SoAd_MainFunctionState
SoAd_MainFunctionTx

This allows optimizing the data reception and data transmission path for a specific use case.

Note: All three MainFunctions must be called from the same task or at least from tasks that do not interrupt each other.

######## 7. SoAdSafeBswChecks

- **UUID**: `8282c8c0-6f7b-4853-86ff-e1f34684d27f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSafeBswChecks`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed. This attribute has to be enabled for safety projects where this component is mapped to an ASIL partition.

**容器描述**:

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal event queues.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon

- **UUID**: `9d6b3fe4-00fd-4b15-a9c1-540da90a9502`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP receptions on socket connections per main function. If more TP receptions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP receptions.

########## 2. SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon

- **UUID**: `ca3110ea-7030-4ce3-95d2-6c1b3d269103`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connection events per main function (e.g. after remote address change). If more socket connection events have to be handled they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses on socket connections state change (e.g. open/close).

########## 3. SoAdEventQueueLimitTcpTxSoCon

- **UUID**: `274896e1-3bb0-418e-86b7-cb713e36a766`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTcpTxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TCP transmissions in case of BSD on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TCP transmissions in case of BSD.

########## 4. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute

- **UUID**: `07433fb8-59ee-4ca8-bb1c-e4dddec489dd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF TxConfirmations on PduRoutes per main function. If more TxConfirmations are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF PduRoutes since TxConfirmation may be called delayed.

This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF Routing Group transmissions.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:

If more events are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for socket handling.

########## 7. SoAdEventQueueLimitLocalAddr

- **UUID**: `866904ef-3d3c-45e5-80d4-29945540aa1b`

If more events are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for local address handling (e.g. slow IP address assignment).

####### 7. SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0c568c61-d9b7-49a6-9cd1-085e5aef3fb3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains a callback to verify if a Rx PDU is allowed to be received.

The callback forwards local socket address, remote socket address, PDU Header ID and a configured number of PDU data to an user. If callback fails SoAd drops the received PDU completely. Otherwise SoAd forwards the received PDU.

Via SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable the feature can be enabled on each socket connection separately.

Hint: This feature is only available for TP-PDUs with PDU header option on TCP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength

- **UUID**: `c242a216-201c-4ff9-afba-c6fe850ac1ef`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of received PDU data which are forwarded to the user for Rx PDU verification.

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPdu

- **UUID**: `34ae0de4-d8e4-4af3-bb09-5e931f747cbd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPdu`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the callback name for the Rx PDU verification.

####### 8. SoAdGeneration

**容器描述**:

Contains the generation configuration parameters of the module SoAd.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **17** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `cc4b1ce4-299b-4084-ae56-a5635c178643`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 2. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `5b7a70fa-f91c-4528-a348-ab9475f1dbc2`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 5. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `7860e17e-d471-4192-978e-5a273d1ad75b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 2. SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `736f51ca-ccf5-420d-8398-f9d2590bae8a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 3. SoAdReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `6179eb43-3818-4bab-82c2-e3cedd1ed1ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceConstantData2Define`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

########## 4. SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `f82c2094-7716-4295-bd80-8e72364fb4df`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.

FALSE: 0:N relational ROM data is not compressed.
TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without increasing the runtime.

########## 5. SoAdReduceDataByStreaming

- **UUID**: `e2b0e431-d22e-40ba-8507-a925c35a3468`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceDataByStreaming`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to pack generated CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data into a data type dependent stream.

FALSE: generated const data is not packed into a data type dependent stream.
TRUE: generated const data is packed into a data type dependent stream.

########## 6. SoAdShortSymbols

- **UUID**: `f2bb9304-4cfd-4588-9b99-fe5cfdce1399`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShortSymbols`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate shortened symbol names.

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

########## 7. SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `e209f3f4-afc2-46cc-92de-d4ee97e5399d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. emty macros.

########## 8. SoAdReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `c32a1835-1f3c-4cbf-a510-1a54c4912438`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReferringKeysInComments`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `d40a0d57-2209-48af-9e31-b5d79d4ef04b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden.
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access.

########## 2. SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `01df6817-6e77-41eb-bdd1-725c395dbe8d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BOOLEAN`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

########## 3. SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `8b6a9646-0482-4399-b545-44e13984665d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

########## 4. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `1892f15e-cddf-4bb4-b8f5-9775cb49e99a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.

####### 9. SoAdSocketApi

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73e78b67-49b4-4ab0-aadf-041657fcc7b9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketApi`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains configuration parameters describing socket API dependent parameter.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTxBufferSize

- **UUID**: `ea5dddd4-329d-4c5f-b5e1-aa48f5127d15`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1452`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a global buffer which is used to store data to be transmitted. This buffer is used for UDP and TCP transmissions. It is recommended to set this value to the maximum transmission unit on SoAd level (MTU without IP and UDP/TCP header).

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

########## 2. SoAdSocketTxBufferNum

- **UUID**: `45952ce2-47da-4211-9c7c-d55a1122fe77`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of global buffer which are used to store data to be transmitted. In case transmission to socket API fails for TCP, SoAd performs a retry for the buffer until the data could be sent or the socket is closed.

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

########## 3. SoAdSocketTxBufferMaxNumPerSocket

- **UUID**: `ca640815-9ec8-4862-b7cc-a0771c9a57e7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferMaxNumPerSocket`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of socket transmission buffer which can be assigned to a single socket at the same time. The transmission request fails for all subsequent transmission requests.

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

########## 4. SoAdSocketRxBufferSize

- **UUID**: `28eb0d1a-849a-4912-af47-46462c2fb16c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRxBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1452`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a global buffer which is used to store received data.This buffer is used for UDP only. It is recommended to set this value to the maximum reception unit on SoAd level (MTU without IP and UDP/TCP header).

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketLingerTimeoutEnabled

- **UUID**: `d67a3ba2-6bfb-4293-8e6c-c4f6e2933eb7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketLingerTimeoutEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Indicates whether the SoAd sets linger time to 0 (SO_LINGER) via setsockopt(). This option can be used to close TCP connections immediately.
true: option is set on socket creation
false: option is not set on socket creation

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is related to the BSD API (e.g. Linux).

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdLocalIpAddrCheckInterval

- **UUID**: `5539fd35-8665-4c84-8282-9aa67765eae1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdLocalIpAddrCheckInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0.1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the interval used to check if a local IP address is available.
This interval can be used to optimze calls to the socket API to prevent timing issues.
If parameter is 0 the check is perfomed in each main function cycle.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketApiType

- **UUID**: `ed73cbb1-3e41-4b32-9058-94e23b3b2ed2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketApiType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `AUTOSAR`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `AUTOSAR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `LINUX` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `QNX` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INTEGRITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the socket API.
AUTOSAR: AUTOSAR compatible API.
LINUX: Linux compatible API (used in Linux operating system).
QNX: QNX compatible API (used in QNX operating system).
INTEGRITY: INTEGRITY compatible API (used in INTEGRITY operating system).

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketReportErrorFunction

- **UUID**: `eb6cc17a-5a13-461c-9a10-a143cd7b7e11`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketReportErrorFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a callback which is called in case any call to socket API returns an unexpected error.

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is related to the BSD API (e.g. Linux).

##### 23. SoAdEventQueueOptimization

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0a8f2b1f-8194-4b73-9a7f-6d2c56b37499`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdEventQueueOptimization`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal event queues.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon

- **UUID**: `9d6b3fe4-00fd-4b15-a9c1-540da90a9502`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP receptions on socket connections per main function. If more TP receptions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP receptions.

######## 2. SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon

- **UUID**: `ca3110ea-7030-4ce3-95d2-6c1b3d269103`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connection events per main function (e.g. after remote address change). If more socket connection events have to be handled they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses on socket connections state change (e.g. open/close).

######## 3. SoAdEventQueueLimitTcpTxSoCon

- **UUID**: `274896e1-3bb0-418e-86b7-cb713e36a766`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTcpTxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TCP transmissions in case of BSD on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TCP transmissions in case of BSD.

######## 4. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute

- **UUID**: `07433fb8-59ee-4ca8-bb1c-e4dddec489dd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF TxConfirmations on PduRoutes per main function. If more TxConfirmations are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF PduRoutes since TxConfirmation may be called delayed.

######## 5. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp

- **UUID**: `36bdf2ff-bb74-4b78-a324-30ae6895e645`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF Routing Group transmissions per main function. If more transmissions are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF Routing Group transmissions.

######## 6. SoAdEventQueueLimitSocketIdx

- **UUID**: `95bad51c-57d6-4bc9-8010-7536cf442e37`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitSocketIdx`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled events on sockets (e.g. TCP socket state changes, reception of data) per main function in case of BSD. If more events are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for socket handling.

######## 7. SoAdEventQueueLimitLocalAddr

- **UUID**: `866904ef-3d3c-45e5-80d4-29945540aa1b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitLocalAddr`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled events on local addresses (e.g. IP address assignment) per main function in case of BSD. If more events are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for local address handling (e.g. slow IP address assignment).

######## 8. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon

- **UUID**: `febee45d-feb1-4223-9188-07a2519a419d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP transmissions on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP transmissions.

##### 25. SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0c568c61-d9b7-49a6-9cd1-085e5aef3fb3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains a callback to verify if a Rx PDU is allowed to be received.

The callback forwards local socket address, remote socket address, PDU Header ID and a configured number of PDU data to an user. If callback fails SoAd drops the received PDU completely. Otherwise SoAd forwards the received PDU.

Via SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable the feature can be enabled on each socket connection separately.

Hint: This feature is only available for TP-PDUs with PDU header option on TCP socket connections.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength

- **UUID**: `c242a216-201c-4ff9-afba-c6fe850ac1ef`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of received PDU data which are forwarded to the user for Rx PDU verification.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPdu

- **UUID**: `34ae0de4-d8e4-4af3-bb09-5e931f747cbd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPdu`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the callback name for the Rx PDU verification.

##### 26. SoAdGeneration

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7495ef7c-ae15-45c9-814e-30aef0f76e60`

- **容器名称**: `SoAdGeneration`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

Contains the generation configuration parameters of the module SoAd.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **17** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `cc4b1ce4-299b-4084-ae56-a5635c178643`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######## 2. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `5b7a70fa-f91c-4528-a348-ab9475f1dbc2`

- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using numerical comparison with other ROM data.

>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using relational comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.

######## 4. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold

- **UUID**: `b116a9c4-d331-4c00-bd88-fca058014897`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold`

######## 5. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold

- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `7860e17e-d471-4192-978e-5a273d1ad75b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

######## 2. SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `736f51ca-ccf5-420d-8398-f9d2590bae8a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

######## 3. SoAdReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `6179eb43-3818-4bab-82c2-e3cedd1ed1ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceConstantData2Define`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

######## 4. SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `f82c2094-7716-4295-bd80-8e72364fb4df`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.

FALSE: 0:N relational ROM data is not compressed.
TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without increasing the runtime.

######## 5. SoAdReduceDataByStreaming

- **UUID**: `e2b0e431-d22e-40ba-8507-a925c35a3468`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceDataByStreaming`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to pack generated CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data into a data type dependent stream.

FALSE: generated const data is not packed into a data type dependent stream.
TRUE: generated const data is packed into a data type dependent stream.

######## 6. SoAdShortSymbols

- **UUID**: `f2bb9304-4cfd-4588-9b99-fe5cfdce1399`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShortSymbols`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate shortened symbol names.

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

######## 7. SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `e209f3f4-afc2-46cc-92de-d4ee97e5399d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. emty macros.
