- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `d40a0d57-2209-48af-9e31-b5d79d4ef04b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden.
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access.

######## 2. SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `01df6817-6e77-41eb-bdd1-725c395dbe8d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BOOLEAN`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

######## 3. SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `8b6a9646-0482-4399-b545-44e13984665d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

######## 4. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `1892f15e-cddf-4bb4-b8f5-9775cb49e99a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.

##### 27. SoAdSocketApi

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73e78b67-49b4-4ab0-aadf-041657fcc7b9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketApi`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains configuration parameters describing socket API dependent parameter.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketTxBufferSize

- **UUID**: `ea5dddd4-329d-4c5f-b5e1-aa48f5127d15`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1452`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a global buffer which is used to store data to be transmitted. This buffer is used for UDP and TCP transmissions. It is recommended to set this value to the maximum transmission unit on SoAd level (MTU without IP and UDP/TCP header).

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

######## 2. SoAdSocketTxBufferNum

- **UUID**: `45952ce2-47da-4211-9c7c-d55a1122fe77`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of global buffer which are used to store data to be transmitted. In case transmission to socket API fails for TCP, SoAd performs a retry for the buffer until the data could be sent or the socket is closed.

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

######## 3. SoAdSocketTxBufferMaxNumPerSocket

- **UUID**: `ca640815-9ec8-4862-b7cc-a0771c9a57e7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTxBufferMaxNumPerSocket`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of socket transmission buffer which can be assigned to a single socket at the same time. The transmission request fails for all subsequent transmission requests.

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

######## 4. SoAdSocketRxBufferSize

- **UUID**: `28eb0d1a-849a-4912-af47-46462c2fb16c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRxBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1452`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a global buffer which is used to store received data.This buffer is used for UDP only. It is recommended to set this value to the maximum reception unit on SoAd level (MTU without IP and UDP/TCP header).

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is based on polling (e.g. Linux).

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketLingerTimeoutEnabled

- **UUID**: `d67a3ba2-6bfb-4293-8e6c-c4f6e2933eb7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketLingerTimeoutEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Indicates whether the SoAd sets linger time to 0 (SO_LINGER) via setsockopt(). This option can be used to close TCP connections immediately.
true: option is set on socket creation
false: option is not set on socket creation

This parameter is only relevant if a socket API is used which is related to the BSD API (e.g. Linux).

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdLocalIpAddrCheckInterval

- **UUID**: `5539fd35-8665-4c84-8282-9aa67765eae1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdLocalIpAddrCheckInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0.1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the interval used to check if a local IP address is available.
This interval can be used to optimze calls to the socket API to prevent timing issues.
If parameter is 0 the check is perfomed in each main function cycle.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketApiType

- **UUID**: `ed73cbb1-3e41-4b32-9058-94e23b3b2ed2`

- **描述**: This parameter defines the socket API.

LINUX: Linux compatible API (used in Linux operating system).
QNX: QNX compatible API (used in QNX operating system).
INTEGRITY: INTEGRITY compatible API (used in INTEGRITY operating system).
